- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Darius I, leader of Persia (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Persia
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Immortal, Replacing Spearman (Requires technology: Bronze Working)
        - Unique Unit: Qizilbash, Replacing Lancer (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Building: Satrap's Court, Replacing Courthouse (Requires technology: Philosophy)
        - Unique Improvement: Charbagh (The Charbagh can be built by Workers on Land with access to Fresh Water, and not next to another Charbagh.
          Each Charbagh yields Food, Production, Gold, and Culture. During a Golden Age, the Charbagh's yields are increased by +1 Production and Culture, in addition to the usual +1 Gold.
          +5% Great People Points in City for each worked Charbagh.); Requires technology: Theology
        - Civilization Ability:  Golden Ages last 50% longer, and 15% of your gross Gold income is converted into Golden Age Points every turn. During a Golden Age, Units receive +1 Movement and +15% Combat Strength.
      - Leader: Darius I
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Plastics: Allows you to build the Research Lab, a building which improves Science in a City.
        Completing it would unlock: Penicillin, Refrigeration
      - Nuclear Fission: Allows you to build the Bazooka, the most powerful ranged Unit and Atomic Bombs, the first nuclear weapon in the game (note: you must first have completed the Manhattan Project and have Uranium available). Also allows you to build the Nuclear Plant, a building which requires Uranium and increases the Production of a City.
        Completing it would unlock: Satellites, Advanced Ballistics
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late game Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Serfdom (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Culture and Faith in Cities for every 4 non-Specialist Citizens.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from Pastures.
      - Divine Right (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +33% Yields from Internal Trade Routes.
        - * Completing an Internal Trade Route triggers a Historic Event as if completing an International Trade Route.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

        - Rationale: TURN 402 EMERGENCY CONTINUES: Maya building Citizen Earth Protocol with 7/7 influential civs = INSTANT CULTURAL VICTORY when completed. All maximum aggression parameters maintained: WarBias/HostileBias/Boldness at 10 ensures immediate declaration of war on Maya and refusal of all peace offers. VictoryCompetitiveness 10 prioritizes stopping cultural victory above all else. Forgiveness 1 ensures no mercy. Meanness 10 ensures maximum demands. Strikes must happen NOW - military positioned at Ecbatana/Kar-Tukulti-Ninurta.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Nuclear Fission
        - Rationale: Manhattan Project completes in 3 turns at Persepolis. Switching to Nuclear Fission immediately will allow atomic bomb production as soon as the project finishes, providing the decisive weapon needed to break Palenque's defenses and stop the Citizen Earth Protocol. This takes priority over Radar/Plastics given the existential timeline.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: None (Policy)
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Marrakech
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: Mombasa: Secure ally maintained at 226 influence. Provides crucial delegate and trade route benefits. Maya gaining influence here (214) after election success - priority to protect against backflip.
        - UpdatedTurn: 271
        
        ### Zuunmod
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: Zuunmod: Despite flipping to Maya (303 vs my 261), maintaining high relationship ensures spy Dabir can continue rigging elections. May reclaim after Bogota/Genoa secured.
        - UpdatedTurn: 271
        
        ### Hanoi
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: Hanoi: Now friends at 95 influence. Maya has 439 as ally. Spy Zenana rigging elections there. Potential future flip target if Bogota consolidation succeeds - this would add another delegate against Maya.
        - UpdatedTurn: 271
        
        ### Melbourne

        ### Mombasa

Must protect this city-state at all costs. Maximum relationship ensures AI prioritizes gold gifts and diplomatic missions to prevent flip.
        - UpdatedTurn: 296
        
        ### Brandenburg

If passed, Brandenburg becomes permanent ally securing delegate regardless of influence. Maximum relationship ensures AI dedicates resources to maintain this alliance while proposal is voted on in 6 turns.
        - UpdatedTurn: 296
        
        ### Rio de Janeiro
        - Public: 100

        - Rationale: Rio de Janeiro (240 influence) - my existing Sphere of Influence resolution is active. This delegate is locked in for World Congress. Maximum relationship maintains stability while I focus on protecting Mombasa from Maya's sphere proposal and securing Brandenburg sphere.
        - UpdatedTurn: 296
        
        ### Bogota
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: Bogota (413 influence) - Maya is at war with Bogota which protects this alliance. However, Sweden is trying to flip this with spy Dabir detected rigging elections. Maintaining maximum relationship to protect this delegate for the World Congress vote.
        - UpdatedTurn: 296
        
        ### Egypt
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Egypt crucial World Congress ally with 4 delegates. They vote with me and their war with Assyria keeps Assyria distracted. Maximum relationship ensures they continue voting against Maya's proposals and support my Open Door Bogota proposal.
        - UpdatedTurn: 341
        
        ### Genoa
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: Genoa delegate lost to France (194 influence vs my 0). Must recover for World Congress vote in 6 turns - Travel Ban proposal needs every vote to block Maya's tourism by 35%. High relationship prioritizes diplomatic missions here despite low treasury (32g).
        - UpdatedTurn: 369
        
        ### Assyria
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Assyria is reduced to 1 city (Halab) with 195 military strength and 100% war weariness. Our war weariness with them is 65%, crippling combat effectiveness. Peace frees military capacity to focus 100% on existential Maya threat and reduces happiness pressure.
        - UpdatedTurn: 386
        
        ### France
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: France war is stalled at 0% with 12% weariness. Setting neutral gives flexibility to focus on Maya. France has defensive pact with Maya but is heavily distracted by Ottoman war. Can deal with France after stopping cultural victory.
        - UpdatedTurn: 388
        
        ### The Maya
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: Maya EXISTENTIAL THREAT INTENSIFIES: Building Citizen Earth Protocol with 7/7 influential civs = instant cultural victory when completed. Palenque has now built AIRPORT making it even more fortified. Maximum hostility maintained to ensure AI declares war TODAY and accepts NO peace until cities captured. Strike force at Ecbatana (8725) and Kar-Tukulti-Ninurta (8220) must assault Palenque/Chichen Itza IMMEDIATELY. War score irrelevant - only objective is capturing the wonder city.
        - UpdatedTurn: 402
        
        ### The Ottomans
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Ottomans remain critical war ally with defensive pact and 11890 military strength. Suleiman provides eastern front distraction against Maya. Maximum relationship ensures continued coordination against common enemy despite his Freedom ideology conflict (-999). His war with Sweden keeps Sweden distracted from my Maya assault.
        - UpdatedTurn: 402
        
        ### Songhai
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: Songhai military strength 11559 absolutely must stay neutral. Their entry on Maya's side would be catastrophic. They just captured Uppsala from Sweden, showing their power. Maximum relationship maintains peace while Persia focuses 100% on eliminating Maya. Do not provoke under any circumstances - I need them as eastern buffer.
        - UpdatedTurn: 402
        
        ### Sweden
        - Public: 0
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: Sweden war has served its purpose with Bogota captured and their military weakened (war score 36%). Peace is necessary to free military supply and attention to focus 100% on the existential Maya threat. War weariness at 18% also hurts happiness.
        - UpdatedTurn: 404
        
        ## Strategy

Nuclear weapon priority increased to 95/95 for immediate deployment once Manhattan Project (3 turns) and Nuclear Fission research complete. Strike forces must assault Palenque/Chichen Itza immediately while preparing tactical nuclear strikes. No peace until Maya cities captured and wonder construction halted.

        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Persian Sphere of Influence: Brandenburg:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Natural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each Natural Wonder provides +2 to all Yields when worked.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Ottoman Sphere of Influence: Palatium:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Endowment for the Arts:
            - Description: Increases up to 33% generation of Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians. Decreases up to 33% generation of Great Scientists, Engineers, and Merchants.
              Civilizations with Scores from Policies and Great Works below the global median will receive a boost to Production and Culture up to 33%.
              Details: The boosts are proportional to the difference between the score of the Civilization and the leader.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Persian Sphere of Influence: Rio de Janeiro:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Mayan Sphere of Influence: Mombasa:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Ottoman Sphere of Influence: Valletta:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Ottoman Sphere of Influence: Hanoi:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Open Door: Genoa:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Travel Ban:
            - Description: Restricts international travel between Civilizations. Reduces the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 35%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Religion: Catholicism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Global Wargames:
            - Description: Begins the Global Wargames project. Once underway, Civilizations can contribute Production towards its completion by selecting it in the city production list. When complete, Civilizations receive bonuses based on how much they contributed.
              : Menin Gate wonder appears in Capital. (Receive +2 Happiness in the City in which it is built. Receive 2 Culture and Golden Age Points whenever an owned unit is killed, scaling with Era. Forts, Citadels and Landmarks provide +2 Culture and Golden Age Points when worked.)
              : Receive a +25% attack bonus to all Military Units for the next 30 turns and 4 of your military units will be maintenance-free.
              : Receive one Infantry and 3 of your military units will be maintenance-free.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Replaceable Parts.
            - Proposer: Persia
          - Enact: International Games:
            - Description: Begins the International Games project. Once underway, Civilizations can contribute Production towards its completion by selecting it in the city production list. When complete, Civilizations receive bonuses based on how much they contributed.
              : Olympic Village wonder appears in Capital. (Receive +3 Happiness and +3 Culture in the City in which it is built. 25% of the Culture from World Wonders and Tiles is added to the Tourism output of the City. All Stadiums receive +2 Gold and Tourism.)
              : Receive a free Great Person of your choice.
              : Tourism increases by 50% for 20 Turns.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Radio.
            - Proposer: The Maya

  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Darius I, leader of Persia (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Persia
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Immortal, Replacing Spearman (Requires technology: Bronze Working)
        - Unique Unit: Qizilbash, Replacing Lancer (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Building: Satrap's Court, Replacing Courthouse (Requires technology: Philosophy)
        - Unique Improvement: Charbagh (The Charbagh can be built by Workers on Land with access to Fresh Water, and not next to another Charbagh.
          Each Charbagh yields Food, Production, Gold, and Culture. During a Golden Age, the Charbagh's yields are increased by +1 Production and Culture, in addition to the usual +1 Gold.
          +5% Great People Points in City for each worked Charbagh.); Requires technology: Theology
        - Civilization Ability:  Golden Ages last 50% longer, and 15% of your gross Gold income is converted into Golden Age Points every turn. During a Golden Age, Units receive +1 Movement and +15% Combat Strength.
      - Leader: Darius I
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Plastics: Allows you to build the Research Lab, a building which improves Science in a City.
        Completing it would unlock: Penicillin, Refrigeration
      - Nuclear Fission: Allows you to build the Bazooka, the most powerful ranged Unit and Atomic Bombs, the first nuclear weapon in the world (note: you must first have completed the Manhattan Project and have Uranium available). Also allows you to build the Nuclear Plant, a building which requires Uranium and increases the Production of a City.
        Completing it would unlock: Satellites, Advanced Ballistics
      - Mobile Tactics: Allows you to build the Mechanized Infantry, a fast and versatile late world Unit that doesn't require any resources.
        Completing it would unlock: Stealth
      
      ## Policies
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Serfdom (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Culture and Faith in Cities for every 4 non-Specialist Citizens.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from Pastures.
      - Divine Right (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +33% Yields from Internal Trade Routes.
        - * Completing an Internal Trade Route triggers a Historic Event as if completing an International Trade Route.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from Boredom in all Cities.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Scientific Revolution (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Can construct Observatories (+6 Science, +1 Science from nearby Mountains, 2 Scientist Slot).
        - * +2 Science from Jungle and Snow.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Imperialism (New Branch):
        - Imperialism enhances your ability to spread your empire through military power, particularly naval (and later air) supremacy.
        - Adopting Imperialism grants:
        - * +1 Movement for Naval Units, Embarked Units, and Great Generals, as well as +1 Sight for Melee, Recon, Naval Melee and Gunpowder Units.
        - * +10% Production toward Military Units, -10% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Each Imperialism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +5% Production for Military Units, -5% Gold required for upgrades.
        - Adopting all policies in Imperialism grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Pentagon.
        - * All Naval and Air units gain the Banzai! Promotion (become stronger as they take damage).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Admirals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Industry (New Branch):
        - Industry provides bonuses to empires focused on Gold and Production.
        - Adopting Industry grants:
        - * +2 Trade Routes.
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Each Industry policy unlocked grants:
        - * -5% Gold needed for purchases.
        - Adopting all Policies in Industry grants:
        - * Unlocks building Broadway.
        - * +3 Happiness per unique owned Luxury Resource.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Specialists.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Merchants with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Authority (New Branch):
        - Authority will greatly benefit warlike and expansionist civilizations.
        - Adopting Authority grants:
        - * +25% combat bonus VS Barbarians and receive announcements when Barbarian Camps spawn in revealed territory.
        - * Receive 25 Culture when you clear Barbarian Camps, scaling with Era.
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 50% of its Strength.
        - * +1 Production in every City.
        - Each Authority policy unlocked grants:
        - * Receive Science and Culture when you kill a Unit equal to 10% of its Strength.
        - Adopting all policies in Authority grants:
        - * Unlocks building Alhambra.
        - * Allows the purchasing of Free Companies, Foreign Legions, and Mercenaries as their prerequisite technologies are researched.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Generals with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

    - inputTokens: 73762
    - reasoningTokens: 7229
    - outputTokens: 306
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Assessing Threats**
            
            I am currently evaluating the overwhelming influence of the Maya, noting their significant delegate count and our cultural inferiority. Their dominance over Sweden requires immediate strategic consideration to prevent further expansion.
            
            **Analyzing Maya's Victory**
            
            My focus has shifted to the Maya's apparent victory condition. Despite having all necessary influential civs, their win is somehow pending. Compounding this, I realize we initiated a war against them, joined by Songhai and several city-states.
            
            **Declaring War on Maya**
            
            I've initiated an all-out global conflict against the Maya to halt their cultural victory. Simultaneously, we are at war with Sweden. My immediate priority is to assess our research and policy choices, particularly the adoption of "Air Supremacy" and available Autocracy tenets.
            
            **Prioritizing Research**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our technology path. Given the ongoing conflict, the immediate free technology from Enlightenment in Rationalism is incredibly appealing. However, I also need to consider the strategic value of Plastics for Research Labs versus Nuclear Fission for advanced units. My current priority is to lock in Enlightenment and then reassess the tech tree with that boost in mind.
            
            **Confirming Research Status**
            
            I'm trying to clarify our research progress. It seems Nuclear Fission may have been unlocked, evidenced by the Manhattan Project's current production, yet it also appears on the research options. I need to definitively confirm its status before proceeding.
            
            **Choosing Research Direction**
            
            My current focus is on selecting our next key technology. I'm weighing the significant science boost from Plastics and its Research Labs against the potential immediate benefits of other paths, especially given our current science output. I've decided to prioritize Plastics for its long-term impact on our research capabilities.
            
            **Assessing Military Gains**
            
            I am pleased to report significant progress on the Sweden front. We are steamrolling their forces in both Alexandria and Thebes, with Alexandria at 55% health. Capturing Stockholm and Thebes would allow us to control multiple capitals, shifting the strategic landscape.
            
            **Securing Capitols**
            
            I am now focusing on the strategic capture of both Stockholm and Thebes from Sweden. It appears Songhai is also aiming for Stockholm, creating a race for its capital. My priority is to secure these locations swiftly, especially considering Sweden's weakened military. Simultaneously, I'm reassessing our proximity and defensive posture against the Maya, noting their capital is relatively close.
            
            **Defending Our Borders**
            
            I'm now assessing immediate threats. Two Maya city-state allies, Zuunmod and Melbourne, have declared war and are dangerously close to our cities. I am actively besieging Zuunmod, and we have a significant military advantage there and at Melbourne, but Mombasa is a more even fight.
            
            **Attacking Maya's Capital**
            
            I'm now assessing our naval strength and positioning to launch an attack on Palenque. Our eastern sea zones are secure, and I'm examining options for the western approach to Maya's capital, which appears to be coastal.
            
            **Launching Naval Assault**
            
            My immediate focus is on deploying our naval forces towards Palenque, assessing both sea and land approaches through Coba. I've realized Coba's proximity to Pasargadae is critical, and our current land operations against Sweden and city-states will be paused to swiftly secure Zuunmod and Melbourne, allowing a full pivot towards Coba and Palenque. I'm also noting the imminent World Congress vote on the Travel Ban.
            
            **Voting on Policies**
            
            I am prioritizing the upcoming World Congress vote, aiming to counter the Maya's proposed tourism boost with our "Global Warworlds" initiative. The immediate benefit of the Menin Gate wonder's attack bonus is crucial given our offensive wars. I'm also noting the Manhattan Project's imminent completion, which will grant us atomic capabilities.
            
            **Avoiding Nuclear Warfare**
            
            I am currently focusing on ethical considerations regarding nuclear weapons. Given the catastrophic and indiscriminate harm they can cause, I've decided to lower our "Nuke" and "UseNuke" flavors to 0. I believe we can achieve victory through conventional military superiority, utilizing our existing strength and Autocracy tenets.
            
            **Setting Strategic Flavors**
            
            I am now adjusting our strategic flavors, prioritizing a high Offense and Mobilization to drive our conquest of Stockholm, Thebes, and Palenque. I've also set Nuke and UseNuke flavors to zero, relying on conventional superiority. Production remains at 100 to accelerate unit and wonder construction.
            
            **Improving Diplomatic Ties**
            
            My current focus is on strengthening diplomatic ties. I'm aiming to improve my relationship with France to potentially draw them into the conflict against the Maya or secure our western flank. I'll also ensure our existing strong alliances with Songhai and The Ottomans remain at maximum.
            
            **Selecting Next Technology**
            
            My attention is now on selecting our next technological advancement. I've decided to proceed with "Plastics" as it provides a crucial science boost through Research Labs, aiding our pursuit of technological parity or superiority.
            
            **Selecting Policy Branch**
            
            I am prioritizing the selection of our next policy branch. Given the significant advantage of a free technology, I am leaning towards "Enlightenment" within the Rationalism branch. This choice seems strategically paramount for accelerating our technological progress in the current global conflict.
            
            **Choosing Policy Branch**
            
            I am prioritizing selecting our next policy branch. Given the significant advantage of a free technology, I am leaning towards "Enlightenment" within the Rationalism branch. This choice seems strategically paramount for accelerating our technological progress in the current global conflict.
            
            **Prioritizing Policy**
            
            I am prioritizing the Rationalism branch for Enlightenment, recognizing the immense value of a free technology in our current conflict. This choice outweighs even the considerable benefits of New World Order for managing a wide empire. I'm also noting Persepolis's upcoming Manhattan Project completion and its potential to shift to producing conventional units and wonders.
            
            **Directing Staff Efforts**
            
            I'm now focusing on optimizing our production and tactical priorities. With immense industrial capacity, I'm setting Offense and Air flavors to 100, and Mobilization to 80, to drive our conquest of Sweden and the Maya. I am also prioritizing air unit production, leveraging our newly adopted "Air Supremacy."
            
            **Directing Production**
            
            My focus is on optimizing our production and tactical priorities to drive our conquest. With immense industrial capacity, I'm setting Offense and Air flavors to 100, and Mobilization to 80, to ensure swift victory. I am also prioritizing air unit production, leveraging our newly adopted "Air Supremacy," alongside a strong naval presence to secure sea lanes and bombard coastal targets.
            
            **Setting Strategic Directives**
            
            I am now consolidating our strategic direction. My immediate actions involve improving relations with France for potential support, prioritizing the research of Plastics for scientific advancement, and adopting the Enlightenment policy to gain a free technology. Concurrently, I am reinforcing our conquest focus by maximizing Offense, Air, and Naval flavors, while completely disengaging from nuclear capabilities.
            
            **Adjusting Strategic Flavors**
            
            I am now fine-tuning our strategic directives. I've set our flavors to maximize Offense, Air, and Mobilization to decisively conquer enemy capitals and prevent a Maya cultural victory, while completely disabling any nuclear capabilities to adhere to ethical warfare. My focus remains on conventional superiority.
            
            **Refining Policy Selection**
            
            I'm now focusing on the exact naming convention for policy selection. Given the options provided, I must use the precise string "Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch)" to ensure the policy is correctly applied. This is a crucial detail for my current strategic planning.
            
            **Selecting Policy**
            
            I'm confirming the exact policy string for selection to ensure accuracy. "Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch)" is the precise choice, aligning with the provided options and guaranteeing its correct application for the free technology benefit.
